appearing in
\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)
and the functional
\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)
includes a negative term
-\delta\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}
. However, if the inertia constant
\varepsilon>0
is sufficiently small, the functionals
\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)
and
\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)
will be both nonnegative. More precisely, we derive the following lemma.

Lemma 3.1
.

If the integer
N\geq 2
and the Leslie’s coefficients satisfy relations (
1.7
) and (
1.38
), then there is a small
\varepsilon_{0}>0
, depending only on
N
,
\beta_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}
, and the all Leslie’s coefficients, such that the energy
\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)
and the energy dissipative rate
\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)
are both nonnegative for any
\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{0})
. Moreover, for all
\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{0})
, we have

(3.7)

\displaystyle\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\thicksim\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\,,

and

(3.8)

\displaystyle\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\thicksim

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\,.

Here the small positive constant
\beta_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}
is given in Proposition
3.1
.

Proof of Lemma
3.1
.

We now find a constant
\varepsilon_{0}>0
such that

(3.9)

\displaystyle\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}\geq 0\,,\ \textrm{and }\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\geq 0

for all
\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{0})
. First, we require the coefficient
\tfrac{-\delta\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}-\tfrac{5}{4}\delta
in
\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)
satisfies

\tfrac{-\delta\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}-\tfrac{5}{4}\delta\geq\tfrac{-\delta\lambda_{1}}{2\varepsilon}>0\,,

which means

(3.10)

0<\varepsilon\leq\tfrac{-2\lambda_{1}}{5}\,.

Noticing that
0<\delta\leq\tfrac{1}{2}
, we have

(3.11)

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}-\delta\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{N}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+(\tfrac{1}{2}-\delta)\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\geq 0\,.

Then by utilizing the Hölder inequality, the Sobolev embedding theory, the Young’s inequality and the bound (
3.3
) in Proposition
3.1
, we estimate

(3.12)

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{+};H^{N})}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{+};H^{N})}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{4}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\beta_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{4}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

for some constant
C=C(N)>0
. Consequently, we deduce

(3.13)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\big(\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}-C\beta_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}\big)\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{4}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\,.

If we require
\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}-C\beta_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}\geq\tfrac{1}{2\varepsilon}
, hence
0<\varepsilon\leq\tfrac{1}{2C\beta_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}}
, then

(3.14)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\tfrac{1}{2\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{4}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\geq 0\,.

As a result, if
0<\varepsilon\leq\min\big\{\tfrac{-2\lambda_{1}}{5},\tfrac{1}{2C\beta_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}}\big\}
, we have
\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\geq 0
.

Next we consider the energy dissipative rate
\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)
. One observes that there is only a negative term
-\delta\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}
in
\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)
under assumption (
1.38
). Via the following elementary estimates

(3.15)

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{|m|\leq N}\Big(\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+2\|(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{|m|\leq N}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+4(1+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}^{2}}{\lambda_{1}^{2}})\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\,,

we know

(3.16)

\displaystyle\tfrac{3\mu_{4}}{8\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}-2\delta\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\big(\tfrac{3\mu_{4}}{8\varepsilon}-8\delta-\tfrac{8\delta\lambda_{2}^{2}}{\lambda_{1}^{2}}\big)\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle+\big(\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}-4\delta\big)\sum_{|m|\leq N}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\,.

If we require
\tfrac{3\mu_{4}}{8\varepsilon}-8\delta-\tfrac{8\delta\lambda_{2}^{2}}{\lambda_{1}^{2}}\geq\tfrac{\mu_{4}}{4\varepsilon}
and
\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}-4\delta\geq\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{2\varepsilon}
, namely,
0<\varepsilon\leq\min\big\{\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{8\delta},\tfrac{\mu_{4}\lambda_{1}^{2}}{64\delta(\lambda_{1}^{2}+\lambda_{2}^{2})}\big\}
, then the quantity in the right-hand side of the inequality (
3.16
) has a lower bound

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mu_{4}}{4\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{2\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\geq 0\,.

In summary, we can take
\varepsilon_{0}=\min\big\{1,\tfrac{-2\lambda_{1}}{5},\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{8\delta},\tfrac{\mu_{4}\lambda_{1}^{2}}{64\delta(\lambda_{1}^{2}+\lambda_{2}^{2})},\tfrac{1}{2C\beta_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}}\big\}>0
, so that
\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\geq 0
and
\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\geq 0
hold for all
\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{0}]
. Moreover, following the previous estimates, one can easily derive the lower bounds of the inequalities (
3.7
) and (
3.8
), and the upper bounds are obviously holds. Then the proof of Lemma
3.1
is finished. ∎

Next we derive the following key energy inequality, which will reduce to the uniform energy bound under the assumption of small size of the initial data. More precisely, we will give the following proposition.

Proposition 3.2
.

Let
N\geq 2
be an integer and assume that
(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})
is a sufficiently smooth solution to the remainder system (
1.20
) on
[0,T]
. Then there are constants
C>0
and
\theta_{0}\gg 1
, depending only on the Leslie coefficients and
\beta_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}
given in Proposition
3.1
, such that

(3.17)

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big[\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\theta_{0}\mathscr{E}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)\Big]+\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\tfrac{\theta_{0}}{2}\mathscr{D}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big[\mathscr{E}^{2}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},\varepsilon}(t)\big]\big[\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\tfrac{\theta_{0}}{2}\mathscr{D}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)\big]

holds for all
t\in[0,T]
and
\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{0}]
, where the small positive constant
\varepsilon_{0}
is mentioned in Lemma
3.1
, and the integer
S_{{}_{\!N}}
is defined in (
1.37
).

Proof of Proposition
3.2
.

For all multi-indexes
m\in\mathbb{N}^{3}
with
|m|\leq N\ (N\geq 2)
, we take the derivative operator
\partial^{m}
on the first equation of the remainder system (
1.20
) and take
L^{2}
-inner product by multiplying
\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}
and integrating by parts over
x\in\mathbb{T}^{3}
. We hence obtain

(3.18)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\mu_{4}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\mu_{1}\big\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\mu_{1}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\sum_{m^{\prime\prime}\leq m^{\prime}}C_{m^{\prime}}^{m^{\prime\prime}}\left\langle(\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\partial^{m^{\prime}-m^{\prime\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}))\partial^{m^{\prime\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}),\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\mathrm{div}\partial^{m}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{u}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle+\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{K}_{\mathrm{u}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle+\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{div}(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{u}}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{u}}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\,,

where we make use of the divergence-free property of
\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}
, the relation
\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}=\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}
and the skew-symmetry of
\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}
.

Acting the derivative operator
\partial^{m}
on the third equation of (
1.20
), taking
L^{2}
-inner product by dot with
\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}
and integrating by parts over
x\in\mathbb{T}^{3}
, we know

(3.19)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{t}\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle+\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\big\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left\langle\partial^{m}(\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla\partial^{m}(\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{1}+\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{2}+\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\,.

Accounting for the cancellation (
2.1
) in Lemma
2.1
, we add the
\frac{1}{\varepsilon}
times of (
3.18
) to (
3.19
), and then deduce that for
|m|\leq N

(3.20)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle+\tfrac{\mu_{4}}{2\varepsilon}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\partial_{t}\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}(\mu_{5}+\mu_{6}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}^{2}}{\lambda_{1}})\|(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\\
m^{\prime\prime}\leq m^{\prime}\end{subarray}}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}C_{m^{\prime}}^{m^{\prime\prime}}\left\langle(\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\partial^{m^{\prime}-m^{\prime\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}))\partial^{m^{\prime\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}),\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{div}(\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{u}}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{u}}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{K}_{\mathrm{u}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\left\langle\partial^{m}(\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla\partial^{m}(\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{1}+\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{2}+\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathcal{G}_{m}\,.

We next deal with the term
\left\langle\partial_{t}\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle
. Straightforward calculation reduces to

(3.21)

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{t}\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle-\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\partial_{t}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle+\left\langle\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}^{2}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\,.

Then, from utilizing the third
\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}
-equation of (
1.20
), we derive that

(3.22)

\displaystyle-\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}^{2}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\left\langle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{1}+\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{2}+\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle\,.

In summary, we obtain the
key
relation to deal with the term
\partial_{t}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})
that

(3.23)

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{t}\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\|\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+2\big\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big)

\displaystyle-\left\langle\sqrt{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\left\langle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{1}+\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{2}+\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle\,.

Consequently, it is derived from the equalities (
3.20
), (
3.23
) and summing up for
|m|\leq N
that

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\big\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+2\sum_{|m|\leq N}\big\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big)

\displaystyle+\tfrac{\mu_{4}}{2\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}(\mu_{5}+\mu_{6}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}^{2}}{\lambda_{1}})\sum_{|m|\leq N}\|(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\\
m^{\prime\prime}\leq m^{\prime}\end{subarray}}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}C_{m^{\prime}}^{m^{\prime\prime}}\left\langle(\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\partial^{m^{\prime}-m^{\prime\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}))\partial^{m^{\prime\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}),\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{K}_{\mathrm{u}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{div}\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{u}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle+\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{div}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{u}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\sqrt{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\mathcal{G}_{m}-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}}\right\rangle

(3.24)

\displaystyle+\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{1}+\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{2}+\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}-\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle\,.

Next we will seek an additional dissipative structure
\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}=\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}
from the term
\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\Delta\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}
in the third
\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}
-equation of the remainder equations (
1.20
). More precisely, we act the derivative operator
\partial^{m}
on
\eqref{Remainder-u-d}_{3}
, take
L^{2}
-inner product by dot with
\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}
and integrate by parts over
x\in\mathbb{T}^{3}
. Then we have for
|m|\leq N

(3.25)

\displaystyle\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{2\varepsilon}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}^{2}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle+\left\langle\partial_{t}\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}((\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{1}+\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{2}+\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\,.

It is easily calculated that

(3.26)

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{t}\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle-\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\partial_{t}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle-\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}((\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\right\rangle\,.

Furthermore, from the integration by parts over
t\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}^{2}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{t}\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle+\left\langle\partial^{m}(\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle-\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial^{m}(\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle-\left\langle\partial^{m}(\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}-\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}-\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle-\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\sqrt{\varepsilon}\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

(3.27)

\displaystyle-\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\sqrt{\varepsilon}\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

Substituting the relations (
3.26
) and (
3
) into (
3.25
) and summing up for
|m|\leq N
reduce to

(3.28)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big(\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}-\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+(\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}-1)\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\Big)+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}-\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}((\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\tfrac{\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}((\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{1}+\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{2}+\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{|m|\leq N}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\sqrt{\varepsilon}\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\sum_{|m|\leq N}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\sqrt{\varepsilon}\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

for all
\varepsilon>0
. Recalling the definition of
\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}}
in (
1.23
), we calculate that

(3.29)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{1\leq|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{1\leq|m|\leq N}\left\langle\lambda_{1}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\lambda_{2}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{1\leq|m|\leq N}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\lambda_{1}(\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}+\lambda_{2}(\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\,,

where the first term in the right-hand side of the previous equality can be bounded by

(3.30)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{1\leq|m|\leq N}\big(|\lambda_{1}|\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}+|\lambda_{2}|\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\big)\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sqrt{c^{\prime}_{0}(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2},N)}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}c^{\prime}_{0}(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2},N)\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{4\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

for some constant
c^{\prime}_{0}(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2},N)>0
. Furthermore, thanks to
\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\mathrm{d}x=|\mathbb{T}^{3}|^{3}<\infty
, we derive from the Gagliardo-Nirenberg interpolation inequality
\|f\|_{L^{6}}\leq C\|\nabla f\|_{L^{2}}
and the Young’s inequality that

(3.31)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\lambda_{1}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{0}+\lambda_{2}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{0},\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}(|\lambda_{1}|+|\lambda_{2}|)\big(\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|\mathrm{d}_{0}|^{3}\mathrm{d}x\big)^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}(|\lambda_{1}|+|\lambda_{2}|)|\mathbb{T}^{3}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}c_{0}^{*}(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2})\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{4\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

for some constant
c_{0}^{*}(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2})>0
. Consequently, we know that

(3.32)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\leq\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}c_{0}(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2},N)\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{2\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{1\leq|m|\leq N}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\lambda_{1}(\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}+\lambda_{2}(\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

holds for
N\geq 2
, where
c_{0}(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2},N)=c_{0}^{\prime}(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2},N)+c_{0}^{*}(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2})>0
.

We now take a small constant

(3.33)

\displaystyle\delta=\min\big\{\tfrac{1}{2},\tfrac{\mu_{4}}{8c_{0}(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2},N)}\big\}\in(0,\tfrac{1}{2}]

such that
\delta c_{0}(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2},N)\leq\tfrac{\mu_{4}}{8}
. Combining the relation (
3.31
), we multiply (
3.28
) by
\delta
and then add it to the (
3
), which gives us

(3.34)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\leq\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(1)}+\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(2)}+\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(3)}+\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}

for
N\geq 2
, where the symbols
\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(i)}

(1\leq i\leq 4)
are defined as

(3.35)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(1)}=\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{K}_{\mathrm{u}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\tfrac{\delta\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}((\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\right\rangle-\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\sqrt{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\\
m^{\prime\prime}\leq m^{\prime}\end{subarray}}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}C_{m^{\prime}}^{m^{\prime\prime}}\left\langle(\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\partial^{m^{\prime}-m^{\prime\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}))\partial^{m^{\prime\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}),\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\delta\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}((\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\right\rangle+\delta\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\delta\sum_{1\leq|m|\leq N}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\sqrt{\varepsilon}\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}},\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\delta\sum_{1\leq|m|\leq N}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\sqrt{\varepsilon}\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

for the functional
\mathcal{K}_{\mathrm{u}}
defined in (
A.3
), and

(3.36)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(2)}=-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{u}},\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{u}},\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

for the expressions
\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{u}}
,
\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{u}}
given in (
A.2
), (
A.7
), respectively, and

(3.37)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(3)}=\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\mathcal{G}_{m}

with the quantity
\mathcal{G}_{m}
mentioned in Lemma
2.1
, and

(3.38)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{1}+\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{2}+\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}-\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\delta\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{1}+\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{2}+\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\tfrac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\sum_{1\leq|m|\leq N}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\lambda_{1}(\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}+\lambda_{2}(\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\,.

Here the vectors
\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}}
,
\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{1}
,
\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}}^{2}
and
\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}}
are determined in (
1.23
), (
A.4
), (
A.5
) and (
A.6
), respectively.

It remains to control the terms
\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(i)}

(1\leq i\leq 4)
for
N\geq 2
. We emphasize that, in the following estimates, we will frequently use the Sobolev interpolation inequality
\|f\|_{L^{6}(\mathbb{T}^{3})}\leq C\|\nabla f\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{3})}
, Sobolev embeddings
H^{1}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{4}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
(or
L^{3}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
),
H^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
and the inequalities (
3.7
), (
3.8
) with the constraint
\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{0})
in Lemma
3.1
. Furthermore, for
|m|\leq N

(N\geq 2)
, the calculus inequality (see
[
20
]
, for instance)

(3.39)

\displaystyle\|\partial^{m}(fg)\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|f\|_{H^{N}}\|g\|_{H^{N}}

will also be frequently utilized. The geometric constraint
|\mathrm{d}_{0}|=1
is also considered in the following energy estimates.

Step 1. Control the term
\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(1)}
.

Via the divergence-free property of
\mathrm{u}_{0}
, we have

(3.40)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{0}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle=-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{0}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{1}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|u_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\leq C\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}

holds for all
|m|\leq N
. If
1\leq|m|\leq N
, we estimate

(3.41)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle=-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{0},\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}\leq C\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}\,.

Moreover, for
m=0
, we have

(3.42)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0},\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\leq\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{\frac{3}{2}}_{L^{2}}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}\,,

where we utilize the Sobolev interpolation inequality
\|f\|_{L^{4}}\leq C\|f\|^{\frac{1}{4}}_{L^{2}}\|\nabla f\|^{\frac{3}{4}}_{L^{2}}
. Then, we obtain

(3.43)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\leq-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

for all
|m|\leq N

(N\geq 2)
. It is easy to derive from the divergence-free property of
\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}
that

(3.44)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial^{m}(\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle=-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Moreover, we can deduce

(3.45)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{div}(\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\odot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\odot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\odot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\odot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\odot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\odot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\big(\|\partial^{m}(\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\odot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{2}}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{m}(\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\odot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{2}}\big)\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}(\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}})\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}+\varepsilon\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\big)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

for all
|m|\leq N
. Recalling the definition of
\mathcal{K}_{\mathrm{u}}
in (
A.3
), we derive from the bounds (
3.40
), (
3.43
), (
3.44
) and (
3.45
) that for
|m|\leq N

(3.46)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{K}_{\mathrm{u}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\leq C\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}+C\big(\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}+\varepsilon\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\big)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

For
|m|\leq N
, we calculate

(3.47)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle=\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla^{2}\mathrm{d}_{0}+\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla^{2}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}+\nabla\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\Big(\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla^{2}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{3}}+\|\nabla\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}\leq C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Via the divergence-free property of
\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}
, we yield that

(3.48)

\displaystyle\tfrac{\delta\lambda_{1}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle=\tfrac{\delta\lambda_{1}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\leq C\varepsilon\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

holds for
|m|\leq N
. Similarly, we deduce from the fact
\mathrm{div}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}=0
that

(3.49)

\displaystyle-\left\langle\partial^{m}(\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sqrt{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m^{\prime}|=1}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\sum_{2\leq|m^{\prime}|\leq|m|}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\leq C\varepsilon\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

for all multi-indexes
m\in\mathbb{N}^{3}
with
|m|\leq N
. For the term
-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\right\rangle
, one estimates that for
|m|\leq N

(3.50)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{m}(\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{|m^{\prime}|=1}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{2\leq|m^{\prime}|\leq|m|}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m}(\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\leq C\varepsilon\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,,

where the fact
\mathrm{div}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}=0
is also utilized.

We derive the bound of the term
-\left\langle\sqrt{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle
as follows:

(3.51)

\displaystyle-\left\langle\sqrt{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left\langle\sqrt{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\partial^{m}(\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial^{m}(\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle+C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{3}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

For all
|m|\leq N
, it is derived that

\displaystyle\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle+\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\left\langle\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{3}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{-C\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}leqm}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\Big(\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+(1+\tfrac{|\lambda_{2}|}{-\lambda_{1}})\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\Big(\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\Big)

(3.52)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

We also can yield that

(3.53)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\\
m^{\prime\prime}\leq m^{\prime}\end{subarray}}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}C_{m^{\prime}}^{m^{\prime\prime}}\left\langle(\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\partial^{m^{\prime}-m^{\prime\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}))\partial^{m^{\prime\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}),\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\\
m^{\prime\prime}\leq m^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}-m^{\prime\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\big(\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{4}_{H^{N+1}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{4}_{H^{N+1}}\big)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

holds for all
|m|\leq N
. For the term
-\delta\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}(\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\right\rangle
, we have

(3.54)

\displaystyle-\delta\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}(\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{3}}\|\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

for
|m|\leq N
. It is easy to estimate

(3.55)

\displaystyle\delta\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{3}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

for
|m|\leq N
. The term
\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\sqrt{\varepsilon}\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle
can be bounded by

(3.56)

\displaystyle\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\sqrt{\varepsilon}\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Via the similar arguments in (
3.56
), we yield

(3.57)

\displaystyle-\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\sqrt{eps}\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Collecting the bounds (
3.46
)-(
3.57
) reduces to

(3.58)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(1)}\leq

\displaystyle C\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}+C\big(\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{4}_{H^{N+1}}\big)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

for all
\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{0})
.

Step 2. Control the term
\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(2)}
.

We need to estimate the terms
-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{u}},\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle
and
-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{u}},\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle
, where the expressions
\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{u}}
and
\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{u}}
are defined in (
A.2
) and (
A.7
), respectively. First, we have

(3.59)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\big[(\mathrm{A}_{0}:(\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\big],\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{A}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\big(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+1}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+1}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

holds for all
|m|\leq N
. We derive from the similar arguments in (
3.59
) that for
|m|\leq N

(3.60)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\big[(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})(\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\big],\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq C\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

For the term
-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\big[(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{0})\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big],\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle
, we estimate

(3.61)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\big[(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{0})\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big],\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\partial^{m}(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\big(\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\big)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Recall that
\mathrm{d}_{0}
obeys the third equation of (
1.10
), hence

(3.62)

\displaystyle-\lambda_{1}(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{0})=\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}+\bm{\gamma}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{0}+\lambda_{2}\mathrm{A}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{0}\,,

where the Lagrangian multiplier
\bm{\gamma}_{0}
is

(3.63)

\displaystyle\bm{\gamma}_{0}=|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}|^{2}-\lambda_{2}\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\,.

Then one can easily yield that if the integer
N\geq 2
,

(3.64)

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}\leq C(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}})(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N}})\,.

and

(3.65)

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}^{2}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}\leq C(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{6}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{6}_{H^{N+2}})\,.

Consequently, we obtain the bound

(3.66)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\big[(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{0})\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big],\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Next, we deduce

(3.67)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\big[(\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{0}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\big],\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\Big(\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{B}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{3}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{3}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{u}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}(\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+1}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{H^{N+1}}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Analogous estimates in (
3.66
) tell us

(3.68)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{3}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\big[\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{0})\big],\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,,

and the similar calculations in (
3.67
) reduce to

(3.69)
