\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{3}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\big[\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes(\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{0}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\big],\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{H^{N+1}}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

It is easy to derive

(3.70)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{5}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\big[(\mathrm{A}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{0})\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\mathrm{A}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\big],\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{A}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

for all
|m|\leq N
. Furthermore, via the analogous estimates in (
3.70
), we imply that

(3.71)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{6}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\big[\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes(\mathrm{A}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{0})+\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes(\mathrm{A}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\big],\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

As a consequence, collecting the estimates (
3.59
), (
3.60
), (
3.66
), (
3.67
), (
3.68
), (
3.69
), (
3.70
) and (
3.71
), we deduce from the definition of
\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{u}}
in (
A.2
) that

(3.72)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{u}},\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\leq C(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

We next estimate the quantity
-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{u}},\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle
. More precisely, we will consider the terms
-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{i-1}\partial^{m}\mathcal{M}_{i},\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle
for
1\leq i\leq 4
. First, we estimate

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\big[(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\big],\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\partial^{m}(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{6}}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}})\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

(3.73)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

The term
-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\big[2(\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\big],\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle
can be controlled by

(3.74)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\big[2(\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\big],\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m\\
m^{\prime\prime}\leq m^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}-m^{\prime\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}))\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}(\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}})(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Via the similar arguments in (
3
) and (
3.74
), we imply that for
|m|\leq N

(3.75)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\big[(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big],\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\big[(2\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})(\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\big],\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

We calculate that

(3.76)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}],\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m}(\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Moreover, it is easily calculated that

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\big[(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{0}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{0})\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big],\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\partial^{m}(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}+\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\partial^{m}((\mathrm{u}_{0}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{\mu_{2}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\partial^{m}((\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{0})\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}(\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}})\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}})(\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}})(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})

(3.77)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

We now estimate that for all

(3.80)

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}(\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}})(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}})

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}(\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}})\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}

), we derive that

(3.94)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{u}},\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathcal{M}_{1}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathcal{M}_{2}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{2}\mathcal{M}_{3}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{3}\mathcal{M}_{4}),\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon^{2}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon^{3}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{3}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon^{4}\mathscr{E}^{2}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})\sum_{p=1}^{4}\mathscr{E}^{\frac{p}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})\big(\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{2}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\big)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

for
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
. Consequently, via the estimates (
3.72
) and (
3.94
), we obtain

(3.95)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(2)}=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{u}},\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{u}},\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})\big(\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{2}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\big)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

for all
|m|\leq N

(N\geq 2)
and for all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}\leq 1
.

Step 3. Control the quantity
\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(3)}
.

Now we carefully estimate the term
\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(3)}
for
N\geq 2
. First, we have

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\lambda_{1}(\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\big(\|(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\tfrac{|\lambda_{2}|}{|\lambda_{1}|}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

(3.96)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Similarly as in (
3
), we imply

(3.97)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\lambda_{2}(\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\leq C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,,

and

(3.98)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}\left\langle\mu_{2}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0,j}\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})_{i}+\mu_{3}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0,i}\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})_{j},\partial^{m}\partial_{j}\mathrm{u}_{R,i}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

It is easy to estimate that

(3.99)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mu_{2}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0,j}\mathrm{d}_{0,k})\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{B}_{R,ki}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{m}\partial_{j}\mathrm{u}_{R,i}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0,j}\mathrm{d}_{0,k})\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{B}_{R,ki}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}\partial_{j}\mathrm{u}_{R,i}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Furthermore, via the analogous calculations in (
3.99
), we imply

(3.100)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mu_{3}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0,i}\mathrm{d}_{0,k})\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{B}_{R,kj}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{m}\partial_{j}\mathrm{u}_{R,i}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mu_{5}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0,j}\mathrm{d}_{0,k})\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R,ki}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{m}\partial_{j}\mathrm{u}_{R,i}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\sum_{m^{\prime}<m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\mu_{6}\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0,i}\mathrm{d}_{0,k})\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R,kj}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{m}\partial_{j}\mathrm{u}_{R,i}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Recalling the definition of
\mathcal{G}_{m}
in (
2.3
) and noticing that
\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(3)}=\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\mathcal{G}_{m}
, we derive from the inequalities (
3
), (
3.97
), (
3.98
), (
3.99
) and (
3.100
) that

(3.101)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(3)}\leq C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

holds for all
N\geq 2
and
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
.

Step 4. Control the term
\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}
.

We first rewrite the expression of
\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}
in (
3.38
) as

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}=

\displaystyle\underset{\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}(\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}})}{\underbrace{-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}}\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underset{\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}(\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{1})}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{1},\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}-\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})+\delta\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underset{\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}(\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{2})}{\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{2},\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}-\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})+\delta\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underset{\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}(\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{2})}{\underbrace{\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}-\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})+\delta\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle}}

(3.102)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underset{\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}(\mathrm{A}\mathrm{B})}{\underbrace{\tfrac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\sum_{1\leq|m|\leq N}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\lambda_{1}(\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}+\lambda_{2}(\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle}}\,.

We next estimate the previous terms one by one. Before doing this, we derive the following inequality, which will be frequently used. More precisely, for all
|m|\leq N
,

(3.103)

\displaystyle\|\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+C\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{3}}+C\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\,.

We initially estimate the quantity
\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}(\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}})
. It is easy to deduce that

(3.104)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\lambda_{1}\partial^{m}(\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\Big(\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{4}}\Big)\|\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,,

where we also make use of the inequality (
3.103
). Similarly as in (
3.104
), we yield that

(3.105)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\lambda_{2}\partial^{m}(\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle\leq C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Based on the definition of
\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}}
in (
1.23
), the inequalities (
3.104
) and (
3.105
) reduces to

(3.106)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}(\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}})\leq C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

For the term
\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}(\mathrm{A}\mathrm{B})
, we estimate that

(3.107)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}(\mathrm{A}\mathrm{B})\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{1\leq|m|\leq N}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}(\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}+\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}})\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\leq C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

We now estimate the quantity
\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}(\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{1})
. First, we have

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle 2\partial^{m}[(\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{2}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{m}\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}})\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}})\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}})\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

(3.108)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

For the term
\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}(|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}|^{2}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle
, one can easily estimate that

(3.109)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}(|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}|^{2}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{m}|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}|^{2}\|_{L^{3}}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}|^{2}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+1}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+1}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+1}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+1}}\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

We then derive the following bound

(3.110)

\displaystyle\tfrac{\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{0}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\Big(\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{3}}+\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{4}}\Big)\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\Big(\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\mathrm{B}_{0}\|_{L^{3}}+\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{B}_{0}\|_{L^{4}}\Big)\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}})\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}})

\displaystyle\times\big(\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Via the analogous arguments in (
3.110
), we imply that

(3.111)

\displaystyle\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{A}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\leq C\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

It is easily estimated that

(3.112)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{m}(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{3}}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}})\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}})\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\times\big(\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Similarly as in (
3.112
), we also have

(3.113)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{2\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\leq C\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Next, we estimate the quantity
-\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{0}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle
for all
|m|\leq N
. Straightforward calculations imply that

(3.114)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{0}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{-\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial_{t}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})-\partial_{t}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\tfrac{-\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\tfrac{-\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\,.

Recalling the constraint (
1.34
), hence
\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}=-\tfrac{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}{2}|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}|^{2}
, we easily derive that

(3.115)

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}=

\displaystyle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})-\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}{2}\partial^{m}|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}|^{2}-\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\,,

which immediately reduces to

(3.116)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})=-\sqrt{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}(\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})

\displaystyle-\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}(\partial^{m^{\prime}}\partial_{t}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\partial_{t}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sqrt{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}\big[\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}-(\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big]

\displaystyle-\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\big[\partial^{m^{\prime}}\partial_{t}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big]

\displaystyle-\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\big[\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}((\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\big]\,.

Then, plugging the relation (
3.116
) into the equality (
3.114
) implies that

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{0}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underset{I_{1}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{-\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}]\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle+\underset{I_{2}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{-\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle+\underset{I_{3}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial_{t}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle+\underset{I_{4}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0},\sqrt{\varepsilon}\partial^{m}(\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle+\underset{I_{5}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial^{m^{\prime}}\partial_{t}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle+\underset{I_{6}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle}}

(3.117)

\displaystyle+\underset{I_{7}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{-\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}[(\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}]\right\rangle}}\,.

For the term
I_{1}
, we estimate

(3.118)

\displaystyle I_{1}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}]\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}+\varepsilon\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}(t))\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

For the term
I_{2}
, it is easily controlled that

(3.119)

\displaystyle I_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}<m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N}})\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N}})

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N}})\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N}})\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

The quantity
I_{3}
can be bounded by

(3.120)

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{|\lambda_{2}|}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{t}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{3}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\partial_{t}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N}})(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}})\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N}})(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Here we make use of the bound

(3.121)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{1}}\leq

\displaystyle C(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N}})(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}})

for
N\geq 2
, which is derived from the
\mathrm{d}_{0}
-equation of (
1.10
). The term
I_{4}
can be estimated as

(3.122)

\displaystyle I_{4}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{|\lambda_{2}|}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m}(\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

For the term
I_{5}
, we calculate that

\displaystyle I_{5}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\partial_{t}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{3}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\partial_{t}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

(3.123)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,,

where the bound
\|\partial_{t}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\leq C(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})
is utilized. We compute the quantity
I_{6}
that

(3.124)

\displaystyle I_{6}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}+\sum_{|m|\leq N}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\partial^{m}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

It is easy to be estimated that

(3.125)

\displaystyle I_{7}\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}[(\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}]\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}\|(\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}+\varepsilon\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}(t))\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Collecting the bounds of
I_{i}

(1\leq i\leq 7)
above, we obtain

(3.126)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{0}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\leq C\varepsilon(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+C(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Recalling that the expression of
\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{1}
in (
A.4
), we derive from the bounds (
3
), (
3.109
), (
3.110
), (
3.111
), (
3.112
), (
3.113
) and (

) that

(3.127)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{1},\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\leq C\varepsilon(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}^{\frac{1}{2}}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+C(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Combining the similar arguments of the inequality (
3.127
) and the bound (
3.103
), we know that

(3.128)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{1},\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\|\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{4}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

We next estimate the quantity
\tfrac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{1},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle
for
N\geq 2
. First, we have

(3.129)

\displaystyle\tfrac{2\delta}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}[(\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

We then estimate

(3.130)

\displaystyle\tfrac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}(|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}|^{2}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\sum_{m^{\prime\prime}\leq m^{\prime}}\|\nabla\partial^{m^{\prime\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\partial^{m^{\prime}-m^{\prime\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{3}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+1}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\leq C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+1}}\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

It is easy to calculate that

(3.131)

\displaystyle\tfrac{\delta\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{0}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\Big(\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{B}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\Big)\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}|\mathbb{T}^{3}|^{\frac{1}{6}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\varepsilon}(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})(\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}})\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,,

where we intrinsically utilize the fact that the volume of
\mathbb{T}^{3}
is finite. Similarly as in (
3.131
), we obtain

(3.132)

\displaystyle\tfrac{\delta\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{A}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\leq C\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,,

and

(3.133)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\delta\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle-\tfrac{2\delta\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq C\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

It remains to estimate the quantity
-\tfrac{\delta\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}((\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle
for all
|m|\leq N
. From the relation (
3.115
), we easily derive that

(3.134)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\delta\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}((\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underset{I\!I_{1}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{\delta\lambda_{2}}{2\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle+\underset{I\!I_{2}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{\delta\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0},\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle}}

\displaystyle+\underset{I\!I_{1}}{\underbrace{\tfrac{-\delta\lambda_{2}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}C_{m}^{m^{\prime}}\left\langle\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle}}\,.

For the term

(3.135)

\displaystyle I\!I_{1}\leq

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}(\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}})

(3.136)

(3.137)

(3.157)

\displaystyle\tfrac{\delta\lambda_{1}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}(\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Similarly as in (
3.157
), we have

(3.158)

\displaystyle\tfrac{\delta\lambda_{2}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\leq C\varepsilon\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

We next calculate that

(3.159)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\delta\lambda_{2}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}[\mathrm{A}_{0}:(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m^{\prime}\leq m\\
m^{\prime\prime}\leq m^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{A}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}-m^{\prime\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{3}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

One easily derives the following bound

(3.160)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\delta\lambda_{2}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}((\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{3}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{C}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}(\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}})(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Via the analogous calculations in (
3.160
), we imply that

(3.161)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\delta\lambda_{2}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}[\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{0}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\leq C(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Furthermore, the similar calculations in (
3.159
) tell us

(3.162)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{\delta\lambda_{2}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle\leq C\varepsilon\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.\,.

Recalling that the definition of
\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{2}
in (

), we imply by collecting the bounds (

), (

), (

(3.163)

\displaystyle\tfrac{\delta}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{2},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+C(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{8}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{8}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

From the inequalities (

3.152
) and (

(3.164)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}(\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{d}}^{2})=

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+C(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{9}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{9}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

We next estimate the quantity
\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}(\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}})
for the integer
N\geq 2
. We start with the term
\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle
for
|m|\leq N
. First, we have

(3.165)

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial^{m}(|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}|^{2}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial^{m}|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}|^{2}\|_{L^{2}}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

Via the inequality (

(3.166)

\displaystyle-\left\langle\partial^{m}(|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}|^{2}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

It is easily derived that

(3.167)

\displaystyle-2\left\langle\partial^{m}[\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{0}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{5}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{5}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

for all
|m|\leq N
and
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
, where the bounds (
3.64
) is also utilized.

For the term
-\lambda_{2}\left\langle\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

(3.168)

\displaystyle-\lambda_{2}\left\langle\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}(\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}})(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{3}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Furthermore, by the similar arguments as in (
3.168
), we immediately have

(3.169)

\displaystyle-\lambda_{1}\left\langle\partial^{m}[\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

(3.170)

We now calculate that

(3.171)

\displaystyle-\lambda_{2}\left\langle\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

|m|\leq N
and

:

(3.172)
