\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,,

where the inequality (
3.103
) is also used. We then estimate that

\displaystyle-2\sqrt{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}[\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}],\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{m}[(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}]\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}[\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})]\|_{L^{3}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle+C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}(\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}})\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{4}_{H^{N+1}}+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{4}_{H^{N+1}})(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

(3.173)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{5}_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{5}_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

for all
|m|\leq N
and
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
, where the last second inequality is implied the bound (
3.103
). Via the same calculations as in the bound (
3.172
), we have

(3.174)

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\left\langle\partial^{m}(|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{0}|^{2}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+1}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

It is easy to deduce that

(3.175)

\displaystyle-\left\langle\partial^{m}[\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0})],\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial^{m}[\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0})]\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+1}})(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+1}})(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,,

where the last second inequality is derived from the bound (
3.64
). We next estimate that

(3.176)

\displaystyle-\left\langle\partial^{m}[\mathrm{u}_{0}\cdot\nabla(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})],\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{0}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{u}_{0}\cdot\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\partial^{m}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{u}_{0}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\sum_{m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{u}_{0}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\cdot\nabla\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\|_{L^{3}}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Furthermore, we have

(3.177)

\displaystyle-\sqrt{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial^{m}[\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})],\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\partial^{m}\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{6}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\sum_{0\neq m^{\prime}\leq m}\|\partial^{m^{\prime}}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\partial^{m-m^{\prime}}\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{L^{4}}\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{3}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Recalling the definition of
\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}}
in (
A.6
) and collecting the previous bounds (
3.165
), (
3.166
), (
3.167
), (
3.168
), (
3.169
), (
3.170
), (
3.171
), (
3.172
), (
3
), (
3.174
), (
3.175
), (
3.176
) and (
3.177
), we obtain

(3.178)

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{5}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{5}_{H^{N+2}})[\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{2}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)]\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{7}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{7}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

for all
|m|\leq N
and
\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{0}]
.

By the similar estimates on the quantity
\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle
in (
3.178
), we obtain

(3.179)

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{5}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{5}_{H^{N+2}})[\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{2}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)]\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{7}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{7}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{6}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{6}_{H^{N+2}})[\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{2}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)]\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{8}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{8}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,,

where we make use of the inequality (
3.103
), hence for
N\geq 2

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N}}\leq C\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\leq C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+1}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Notice that the bound

(3.180)

\displaystyle\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}}|\mathbb{T}^{3}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\leq C\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}}\leq C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

holds for all
|m|\leq N
. Then, from the similar arguments in (
3.178
) and the previous bound, we deduce that

(3.181)

\displaystyle\delta\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{5}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{5}_{H^{N+2}})[\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{2}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)]\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{7}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{7}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\|\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{5}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{5}_{H^{N+2}})[\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{2}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)]\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{7}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{7}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,.

Finally, from the inequalities (
3.178
), (
3.179
) and (
3.181
), we derive that

(3.182)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}(\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}})=

\displaystyle\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}},\partial^{m}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}-\partial^{m}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})+\delta\partial^{m}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{6}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{6}_{H^{N+2}})[\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{2}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)]\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{8}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{8}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

holds for all
N\geq 2
and
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
.

As a consequence, substituting the inequalities (
3.106
), (
3.107
), (
3.140
), (
3.164
) and (
3.182
) into (
3
) reduces to

(3.183)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{N}^{(4)}\leq

\displaystyle C(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{9}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{9}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{6}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{6}_{H^{N+2}})[\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{2}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)]\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{8}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{8}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

for all
N\geq 2
and
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
.

Step 5. Close the a priori uniform energy estimates.

Via plugging the bounds (
3.58
), (
3.95
), (
3.101
) and (
3.183
) into the relation (
3.34
), we obtain

(3.184)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{9}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{9}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{6}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{6}_{H^{N+2}})[\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{2}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)]\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{8}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{8}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}(1+\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{3}_{H^{N+2}})(\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|_{H^{N+2}})\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

for all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}\leq 1
. Furthermore, by the relations (
3.2
) and the bound (
3.3
) we know that

(3.185)

\displaystyle\|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}}\leq c_{0}^{-1}\mathscr{E}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)\leq C(\beta_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0})\,,

\displaystyle\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}}+\|\Delta\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{N+2}}\leq c_{0}^{-1}\mathscr{D}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)\,.

As a result, we deduce that for all
N\geq 2
and
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}

(3.186)

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+C\big(\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\big)\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\big(\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{2}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\big)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\,,

which immediately implies by the Young’s inequality that

(3.187)

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\leq

\displaystyle C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\big(\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{2}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\big)\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle C\mathscr{D}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)+C\big(\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)\big)\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)

for all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
. Taking a large constant
\theta_{0}\gg 1
and adding the
\theta_{0}
times of the differential inequality (
3.4
) to the previous inequality gives us

(3.188)

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big[\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\theta_{0}\mathscr{E}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)\Big]+\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\tfrac{\theta_{0}}{2}\mathscr{D}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big[\mathscr{E}^{2}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},\varepsilon}(t)\big]\big[\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\tfrac{\theta_{0}}{2}\mathscr{D}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)\big]

for all
N\geq 2
and
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
. Then the proof of Proposition
3.2
is finished.
∎

4.
Global well-posedness of the remainder system: proof of Theorem
1.1

.

In this section, we aim at completing the proof of Theorem
1.1
. Without loss of generality, based on the a priori energy estimate (
3.17
) derived in Proposition
3.2
, we prove the global existence of the remainder system (
1.33
) with the initial data (
1.35
). Then, combining the solution
(\mathrm{u}_{0},\mathrm{d}_{0})
to the limit equations (
1.10
) with initial conditions (
1.13
) given in Proposition
3.1
, we know that

(\mathrm{u}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{d}^{\varepsilon})=(\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{d}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})

obeys the first three equations of the system (
1.1
) with the initial data (
1.8
). We remark that the similar arguments will also justify the global existence to the remainder system (
1.20
)-(
1.27
), which is the remainder system with respect to the ill-posedness initial data. Thus, we obtain a global classical solution

\displaystyle(\mathrm{u}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{d}^{\varepsilon})=(\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{d}_{0}+\varepsilon\mathrm{D}_{I}^{\varepsilon}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})

to the original system (
1.1
)-(
1.8
). We omit the details of this case here.

We now introduce a mollifier over the periodic space variable. Recall that
\mathbb{T}^{3}=\mathbb{R}^{3}/\mathbb{L}^{3}
, where
\mathbb{L}^{3}\subset\mathbb{R}^{3}
is some
3
-dimensional lattice. Let
\varphi\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
be such that
\varphi\geq 0
,
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\varphi(x)\mathrm{d}x=1
, and
\varphi(x)=0
for
|x|>1
. We then define
\varphi^{\zeta}(x)\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{3})
by

\displaystyle\varphi^{\zeta}(x)=\tfrac{1}{\zeta^{3}}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{L}^{3}}\varphi\left(\tfrac{x+l}{\zeta}\right)

for any
\zeta>0
. Then we define a mollifier
\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}
as

\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}f(x)=\varphi^{\zeta}*f(x)=\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}\varphi^{\zeta}(x-y)f(y)\mathrm{d}y\,.

Next we prove the main results of this paper.

Proof of Theorem
1.1
.

We first construct the following approximate system of the remainder equations (
1.20
)-(
1.27
)

(4.1)

\left\{\begin{array}[]{r}\partial_{t}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}-\tfrac{1}{2}\mu_{4}\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\Delta\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}+\nabla p_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}=\mu_{1}\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{div}\big((\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{A}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\big)\\[5.69054pt]
\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathcal{K}_{\mathrm{u},\zeta}+\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{div}\big(\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{u},\zeta}+\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{u},\zeta}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{u},\zeta}\big)\,,\\[8.53581pt]
\mathrm{div}\,\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}=0\,,\\[8.53581pt]
\partial_{t}(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R,\varepsilon}^{\varepsilon})_{\zeta}+\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\big[(\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R,\varepsilon}^{\varepsilon})_{\zeta}\big]\\[5.69054pt]
\qquad\qquad\qquad+\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R,\varepsilon}^{\varepsilon})_{\zeta}-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\Delta\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{d}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{t}(\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{u}_{R,\varepsilon}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\\[5.69054pt]
\qquad\qquad\qquad=\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d},\zeta}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathcal{S}^{1}_{\mathrm{d},\zeta}+\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathcal{S}^{2}_{\mathrm{d},\zeta}+\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d},\zeta}\,,\\[8.53581pt]
\partial_{t}\mathrm{d}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}=(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R,\varepsilon}^{\varepsilon})_{\zeta}-\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\big((\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{d}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}\big)\end{array}\right.

with the initial conditions

(4.2)

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{d}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon},(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R,\varepsilon}^{\varepsilon})_{\zeta}\big)\big|_{t=0}=(\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon,in},\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon,in},\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\widetilde{\mathrm{d}}_{R}^{\varepsilon,in})\,,

where

\mathrm{A}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}=\tfrac{1}{2}(\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}+(\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon})^{\top})\,,\ \mathrm{B}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}=\tfrac{1}{2}(\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}-(\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon})^{\top})\,,

and the symbols
\mathcal{K}_{\mathrm{u},\zeta}
,
\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{u},\zeta}
,
\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{u},\zeta}
,
\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{u},\zeta}
,
\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d},\zeta}
,
\mathcal{S}^{1}_{\mathrm{d},\zeta}
,
\mathcal{S}^{1}_{\mathrm{d},\zeta}
and
\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d},\zeta}
are the same form as
\mathcal{K}_{\mathrm{u}}
,
\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{u}}
,
\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{u}}
,
\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{u}}
,
\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}}
,
\mathcal{S}^{1}_{\mathrm{d}}
,
\mathcal{S}^{1}_{\mathrm{d}}
and
\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}}
, respectively (just replacing the symbols
\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}
,
\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}
and
\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}
with the corresponding symbols
\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}
,
\mathrm{d}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}
and
(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R,\varepsilon}^{\varepsilon})_{\zeta}
). The previous approximate system can be regarded as an ordinary differential equation in
H^{N}
for any
\varepsilon>0
by verifying the conditions of Cauchy-Lipschitz theorem. Thus it admits a unique solution
(\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{d}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon})
in
C([0,T_{\varepsilon,\zeta});H^{N})
with the maximal time interval
[0,T_{\varepsilon,\zeta})
.

We define the following approximate energy
\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}^{\zeta}(t)
and the approximate energy dissipative rate
\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}^{\zeta}(t)
:

(4.3)

\displaystyle\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}^{\zeta}(t)=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+(1-\delta)\|(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R,\varepsilon}^{\varepsilon})_{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{d}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(\tfrac{-\delta\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}-\tfrac{5}{4}\delta)\|\mathrm{d}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\|\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\delta}{2}\mathrm{d}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\delta\|(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R,\varepsilon}^{\varepsilon})_{\zeta}+\mathrm{d}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\sum_{|m|\leq N}\left\langle\partial^{m}(\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}),\partial^{m}(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R,\varepsilon}^{\varepsilon})_{\zeta}\right\rangle\,,

and

(4.4)

\displaystyle\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}^{\zeta}(t)=

\displaystyle\tfrac{3\mu_{4}}{8\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{\delta}{2\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{d}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}-\delta\|(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R,\varepsilon}^{\varepsilon})_{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mu_{1}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\|(\partial^{m}\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{A}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}):\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}(\mu_{5}+\mu_{6}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}^{2}}{\lambda_{1}})\sum_{|m|\leq N}\|(\partial^{m}\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{A}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\|\partial^{m}(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R,\varepsilon}^{\varepsilon})_{\zeta}+(\partial^{m}\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{B}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{A}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\,,

where the constant
\delta\in(0,\tfrac{1}{2}]
is the same as that mentioned in
\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)
and
\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)
. As shown in Lemma
3.1
, when
N\geq 2
and
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
, the approximate energy
\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}^{\zeta}(t)
and the approximate energy dissipative rate
\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}^{\zeta}(t)
are nonnegative. Moreover,

(4.5)

\displaystyle\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}^{\zeta}(t)\thicksim\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\|(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R,\varepsilon}^{\varepsilon})_{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{d}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{d}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}\,,

and

(4.6)

\displaystyle\mathscr{D}_{N,\varepsilon}^{\zeta}(t)\thicksim

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{d}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\|(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R,\varepsilon}^{\varepsilon})_{\zeta}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|m|\leq N}\|\partial^{m}(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R,\varepsilon}^{\varepsilon})_{\zeta}+(\partial^{m}\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{B}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\tfrac{\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}}(\partial^{m}\mathcal{J}_{\zeta}\mathrm{A}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\,.

Via the similar arguments in Proposition
3.2
, we can derive that for all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
,
\zeta>0
and
t\in[0,T_{\varepsilon,\zeta})

(4.7)

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}

\displaystyle\Big[\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\theta_{0}\mathscr{E}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)\Big]+\mathscr{D}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\tfrac{\theta_{0}}{2}\mathscr{D}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big[(\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t))^{2}+(\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t))^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)\big]\big[\mathscr{D}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\tfrac{\theta_{0}}{2}\mathscr{D}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)\big]\,,

where the constant
C>0
,
\varepsilon_{0}\in(0,1]
and
\theta_{0}\gg 1
are mentioned in Proposition
3.2
.

Next we prove that the maximal time
T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}=+\infty
under the small size constraint of the initial energy
E^{in}
, defined in (
1.39
). More precisely, there is a small
\xi_{0}>0
, independent of
\varepsilon
and
\zeta
, such that if
E^{in}\leq\xi_{0}
, we have
T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}=+\infty
and

(4.8)

\displaystyle\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\theta_{0}\mathscr{E}_{S_{{}_{\!N}},0}(t)+\tfrac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\big[\mathscr{D}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(\tau)+\tfrac{\theta_{0}}{2}\mathscr{D}_{S_{{}_{\!{}_{N}}},0}(\tau)\big]\mathrm{d}\tau\leq(1+\theta_{0})\xi_{0}

holds for all
t\geq 0
,
\zeta>0
and
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
.

Assume that
T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}<+\infty
, We define a time number
T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}^{*}
as

(4.9)

\displaystyle T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}^{*}=\sup\Big\{\tau\in[0,T_{\varepsilon,\zeta});\sup_{t\in[0,\tau]}C\big[(\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t))^{2}+(\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t))^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)\big]\leq\tfrac{1}{2}\Big\}\in[0,T_{\varepsilon,\zeta})\,.

By Lemma
3.1
and the definition of the initial energy
E^{in}
in (
1.39
), we have

(4.10)

\displaystyle C\big[(\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(0))^{2}+(\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(0))^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(0)\big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big[(C_{2}E^{in})^{2}+2\sqrt{C_{2}E^{in}}\big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big[(C_{2}\xi_{0})^{2}+2\sqrt{C_{2}\xi_{0}}\big]

holds for all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
, where the constants
C_{2}
and
\varepsilon_{0}
are given in Lemma
3.1
. As listed in Proposition
3.1
of this paper, we first require the number
\xi_{0}\leq\beta_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}
such that the initial energy bound
E^{in}\leq\xi_{0}
guarantees the global existence results of Wang-Zhang-Zhang
[
21
]
to the limit system (
1.10
) with the initial data (
1.13
). We thus choose the small positive constant
\xi_{0}\in(0,\beta_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}]
such that

C\big[(C_{2}\xi_{0})^{2}+2\sqrt{C_{2}\xi_{0}}\big]\leq\tfrac{1}{4}\,.

Specifically, one can take any
\xi_{0}\in(0,\min\{1,\beta_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0},\tfrac{1}{C_{2}}\tfrac{1}{144C^{2}C_{2}}\}]\subset(0,1]
. As a result, we know that if
E^{in}\leq\xi_{0}
, then for all
\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{0}]

(4.11)

\displaystyle C\big[(\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(0))^{2}+(\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(0))^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(0)\big]\leq\tfrac{1}{4}\,.

By the initial energy bound (
4.11
) and the continuity of the energy functional
\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)
in
t\in[0,T_{\varepsilon,\zeta})
, we imply that
T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}^{*}>0
.

We now claim that there is a small
\xi_{0}\in(0,\min\{1,\beta_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0},\tfrac{1}{C_{2}}\tfrac{1}{144C^{2}C_{2}}\}]
such that if
E^{in}\leq\xi_{0}
, then
T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}^{*}=T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}
holds for all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
and
\zeta>0
. Indeed, if
T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}^{*}<T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}
for all
\xi_{0}\in(0,\min\{1,\beta_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0},\tfrac{1}{C_{2}}\tfrac{1}{144C^{2}C_{2}}\}]
, the inequality (
4.7
) tells us that

(4.12)

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}

\displaystyle\Big[\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\theta_{0}\mathscr{E}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)\Big]+\tfrac{1}{2}\big[\mathscr{D}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\tfrac{\theta_{0}}{2}\mathscr{D}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)\big]\leq 0

holds for all
t\in[0,T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}^{*}]
and for all
\zeta>0
,
\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{0}]
. Hence, integrating the previous differential inequality over
[0,t]
reduces to

(4.13)

\displaystyle\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}^{\zeta}(t)+\theta_{0}\mathscr{E}_{S_{{}_{\!N}},0}(t)+\tfrac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\big[\mathscr{D}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(\tau)+\tfrac{\theta_{0}}{2}\mathscr{D}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(\tau)\big]\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}^{\zeta}(0)+\theta_{0}\mathscr{E}_{S_{{}_{\!N}},0}(0)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+\theta_{0})\big[\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}^{\zeta}(0)+\mathscr{E}_{S_{{}_{\!N}},0}(0)\big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+\theta_{0})C_{2}\xi_{0}

holds for all
t\in[0,T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}^{*}]
and for all
\zeta>0
,
\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{0}]
, which means that

(4.14)

\displaystyle\mathscr{E}_{N,\varepsilon}^{\zeta}(t)+\mathscr{E}_{S_{{}_{\!N}},0}(t)\leq(1+\theta_{0})C_{2}\xi_{0}\,.

If we choose a fixed
\xi_{0}=\min\{1,\beta_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0},\tfrac{1}{C_{2}(1+\theta_{0})}\tfrac{1}{144C^{2}C_{2}(1+\theta_{0})}\}\in(0,\min\{1,\beta_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0},\tfrac{1}{C_{2}}\tfrac{1}{144C^{2}C_{2}}\}]
, then the previous energy bound implies that under the constraint
E^{in}\leq\xi_{0}

(4.15)

\displaystyle C\big[(\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t))^{2}+(\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t))^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)\big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big[(C_{2}(1+\theta_{0})\xi_{0})^{2}+2\sqrt{C_{2}(1+\theta_{0})\xi_{0}}\Big]\leq\tfrac{1}{4}<\tfrac{1}{2}

holds for all
t\in[0,T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}^{*}]
. Thus the continuity of
\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)
yields that there exists a
t^{*}>T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}^{*}
such that

\sup_{t\in[0,t^{*}]}C\big[(\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t))^{2}+(\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t))^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)\big]\leq\tfrac{1}{2}\,,

which contradicts to the definition of
T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}^{*}
. Consequently, we have
T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}^{*}=T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}<+\infty
.

Therefore, it must hold that at time
t=T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}

(4.16)

\displaystyle\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}_{S_{\!{}_{N}},0}(t)<+\infty\,.

We then can extend the solution
(\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{d}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon})
of the approximate system (
4.1
) to a lager interval
[0,T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}+\kappa)
for some
\kappa>0
. This contradicts to the maximality of
T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}
. As a consequence, there is a small
\xi_{0}>0
, independent of
\varepsilon
and
\zeta
, such that if
E^{in}\leq\xi_{0}
for all
\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{0}]
, we have
T_{\varepsilon,\zeta}=+\infty
and

(4.17)

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 0}\big[\mathscr{E}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(t)+\mathscr{E}_{S_{{}_{\!N}},0}(t)\big]+\tfrac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\big[\mathscr{D}^{\zeta}_{N,\varepsilon}(\tau)+\tfrac{1}{2}\mathscr{D}_{S_{{}_{\!{}_{N}}},0}(\tau)\big]\mathrm{d}\tau\leq(1+\theta_{0})\xi_{0}

holds for all
\zeta>0
and
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
.

Then, by compactness arguments (let
\zeta\rightarrow 0
), we get vector field
(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
satisfying

\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\in L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{+};H^{N})\,,\ \nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+};H^{N})

for all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
, which solves the remainder system (
1.20
)-(
1.27
). Moreover,
(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon},\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})
obeys the energy bound

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 0}\left(\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R,\zeta}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N+1}}\right)(t)+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{\infty}\|\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N}}(t)\mathrm{d}t\leq C\xi_{0}

for some
C>0
, which is uniform in
\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{0}]
. It is easy to know that

\displaystyle 2(\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})(0,x)+\sqrt{\varepsilon}|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}|^{2}(0,x)=0\,.

As a consequence, Lemma
2.2
(or Remark
2.1
) implies the constraint (
1.34
) holds at any time.Then the proof of Theorem
1.1
is finished.
∎

Appendix A
Remainder systems

In this section, we will present the tedious terms of the remainder system (
1.20
). After submitting the ansatz (
1.15
) into the hyperbolic Ericksen-Leslie’s liquid crystal model (
1.1
), one obtain the reminder system (
1.20
), namely,

\left\{\begin{array}[]{c}\partial_{t}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}-\tfrac{1}{2}\mu_{4}\Delta\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\nabla p_{R}^{\varepsilon}=\mu_{1}\mathrm{div}\big[(\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\big]\\
\qquad\qquad+\mathcal{K}_{\mathrm{u}}+\mathrm{div}(\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{u}}+\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{u}}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{u}})+\varepsilon\mathrm{div}\mathcal{Q}_{\mathrm{u}}(\mathrm{D}_{I})\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\mathrm{div}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}=0\,,\\[5.69054pt]
\mathrm{D}^{2}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\tfrac{-\lambda_{1}}{\varepsilon}\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\Delta\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\partial_{t}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{D}_{I}^{\varepsilon})\\
=\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}}+\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathcal{S}^{1}_{\mathrm{d}}+\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathcal{S}^{2}_{\mathrm{d}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}}+\mathcal{Q}_{\mathrm{d}}(\mathrm{D}_{I})\end{array}\right.

with the constraint

(A.1)

\displaystyle 2\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot(\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{D}_{I}^{\varepsilon})+\sqrt{\varepsilon}|\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{D}_{I}^{\varepsilon}|^{2}=0\,,

where the tensor
\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{u}}
is defined in (
1.22
) and the vector field
\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{d}}
is given in (
1.23
), the linear tensor term
\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{u}}
is

(A.2)

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{u}}=

\displaystyle\mu_{1}\big[(\mathrm{A}_{0}:(\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}))\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}+(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\big]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mu_{2}\big[(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{0})\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\big]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mu_{3}\big[\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{0})+\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes(\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\big]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mu_{5}\big[(\mathrm{A}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{0})\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\mathrm{A}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}\big]+\mu_{6}\big[\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes(\mathrm{A}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{0})+\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes(\mathrm{A}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\big]\,,

the linear vector field
\mathcal{K}_{\mathrm{u}}
is

(A.3)

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{\mathrm{u}}=

\displaystyle-\mathrm{u}_{0}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}-\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{u}_{0}-\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle-\mathrm{div}(\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\odot\nabla_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\odot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\odot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\,,

the singular linear term
\mathcal{S}^{1}_{\mathrm{d}}
is of the form

(A.4)

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}^{1}_{\mathrm{d}}=

\displaystyle 2(\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}|^{2}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\lambda_{1}(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lambda_{2}\big[\mathrm{A}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}-(\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{0}-(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}-2(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\big]\,,

the singular nonlinear term
\mathcal{S}^{2}_{\mathrm{d}}
is defined as

(A.5)

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}^{2}_{\mathrm{d}}=

\displaystyle|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}|^{2}\mathrm{d}_{0}+2(\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}-\mathrm{D}^{2}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}-|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}|^{2}\mathrm{d}_{0}+\lambda_{1}\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lambda_{2}\big[\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}-\mathrm{A}_{0}:(\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}-(\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big]

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\lambda_{2}\big[\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:(\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\big]\,,

the nonsingular nonlinear term
\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}}
is

(A.6)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{d}}=

\displaystyle(|\nabla\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}|^{2}-|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}|^{2})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}-2\big[\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\big]\mathrm{d}_{0}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\lambda_{2}\big[(\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:(\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big]

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sqrt{\varepsilon}\big[|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}|^{2}\mathrm{d}_{0}+2\lambda_{2}(\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle+2\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0}\cdot(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big]

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\varepsilon|\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}|^{2}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}}\mathrm{d}_{0})-\mathrm{u}_{0}\cdot\nabla(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})-\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla(\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0})\,,

and the nonsingular nonlinear tensor
\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{u}}
is

(A.7)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{u}}=\mathcal{M}_{1}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathcal{M}_{2}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{2}\mathcal{M}_{3}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}^{3}\mathcal{M}_{4}\,.

Here the term
\mathcal{M}_{1}
is

(A.8)

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{1}=

\displaystyle\mu_{1}\big[(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+2\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}+(\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle+(2\mathrm{A}_{0}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{A}_{R}^{\varepsilon}:\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0})(\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\big]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mu_{2}\big[(\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})\otimes\mathrm{d}_{0}+(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{0})\otimes\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\mu_{3}\big[\mathrm{d}_{0}\otimes(\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon})+\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\otimes(\mathrm{D}_{\mathrm{u}_{0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{u}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{d}_{0}+\mathrm{B}_{0}\mathrm{d}_{R}^{\varepsilon}+\mathrm{B}_{R}^{\varepsilon}\mathrm{d}_{0})\big]
